# English-Chinese Word Pairs for CCL Pronunciation Trainer

This file contains English-Chinese word/phrase pairs extracted from merged-conversations.md, organized by conversation ID to match the structure of words.md.

**Source:** Extracted from merged-conversations.md
**Format:** English word/phrase | Chinese translation | UK Pronunciation | US Pronunciation
**Total Conversations:** 96 (70253 → 70158)

**Pronunciation Guide:**
- **UK Pronunciation:** British English pronunciation with IPA notation and phonetic spelling
- **US Pronunciation:** American English pronunciation with IPA notation and phonetic spelling
- **Format:** /IPA notation/ — sounds like **PHONETIC-SPELLING**
